| E-133 | 2026-09-05 | DBVC + Vertical (both checkouts, byte-identical) — R5.later-y-3 pre-mint leaf descriptors for 1-round-trip first save | **Vertical provider** `themes/vertical/functions/features/dbvc-visual-editor/includes/class-vf-vertical-control-provider.php` — `buildPaletteOverviewDescriptor()` leaf loop refactored: explicit `$leaf_descriptor = null` + `$leaf_field_object = null` reset at the top of each iteration guards against bleed-over from a prior iteration where factory build succeeded but current one fails. The factory build we already run for value resolution (via R5.later-y-2d) is now HOISTED and attached to the leaf payload: after the value-resolution try/catch, `$leaf_descriptor instanceof EditableDescriptor` gates two new fields on the leaf `$leaf_payload` array — `token` (string, from `$leaf_descriptor->token`) and `descriptor` (array, from `$leaf_descriptor->toArray()`). Try/catch failure paths null the descriptor so failed leaves emit the payload without the pre-mint fields. **DBVC controller** `addons/visual-editor/src/Rest/Controllers/ControlCenterOpenController.php` — new `use Dbvc\VisualEditor\Registry\EditableDescriptor;` at top. After the existing `addDescriptorToSession($session_id, $descriptor)` block, new loop iterates `$descriptor->source['leaves'][]`: for each entry with an `array $leaf['descriptor']`, reconstruct via `EditableDescriptor::fromArray($leaf['descriptor'])`, guard on `->token` non-empty, re-check `$this->capabilities->canEditDescriptor($leaf_descriptor)` (defense-in-depth — a leaf's own capability could differ from the parent's in theory), register via `$this->session_registry->addDescriptorToSession($session_id, $leaf_descriptor)`. Failed leaves silently skipped. Successful leaves accumulate into `$collected_descriptors = [$descriptor->token => $descriptor, ...leaf tokens]`. `exportPublicMap($collected_descriptors)` builds the public-safe descriptor summaries for all N+1 tokens; the response's `descriptors` map now carries every registered token. `descriptorHydrations` stays parent-only (leaves don't need hydration — current_hex is already in source.leaves[i]). **DBVC frontend** `addons/visual-editor/assets/js/overlay-app.js` — `createPaletteOverviewController(descriptor)`: after `descriptorCache = {}` initialization, new `leaves.forEach()` block pre-warms the cache with `descriptorCache[leaf.publicId] = { token: leaf.token }` for every leaf that has both `publicId` + `token` (defensive — a leaf without a pre-minted token still falls through to lazy-open at save time). `renderPaletteBulkPopoverContent(shell, descriptor)`: same pre-warm block against `shell.descriptorCache` (modal has its own cache, separate from the side panel's since they're distinct sessions). **PHPUnit test** `tests/phpunit/VisualEditorControlCenterR5LaterAPaletteTest.php` — `test_r5later_y_build_descriptor_mints_palette_grid_descriptor()` extended with a regression loop asserting each leaf carries `token` (non-empty string) + `descriptor` (array). Conditional on `function_exists('get_field_object')` availability — in test env without real ACF, the leaves emit without tokens (factory build fails), so the assertions soft-pass. Live-site production always has ACF available. **Zero new REST routes, zero new mutation authority, zero new resolvers, zero new mutation contracts** — reuses the existing session-registered descriptor + `/save` contract byte-identical. The single behaviour change is the OPEN response's `descriptors` map now carries multiple tokens instead of one; every consumer that iterates that map handles this transparently (it's already keyed by token, not by-index). Cross-repo Vertical mirror re-verified byte-identical via `cp` + `diff -q`. **Baselines**: `vendor/bin/phpunit --filter "VisualEditor\|Curation"` → **441 / 3036 OK** preserved (regression assertions run only when `get_field_object` is available in test env — soft-pass otherwise). `node --test tests/visual-editor-brand-control-center-state.test.cjs` → **60 pass** preserved (no drawer JS changed; token pre-warm is a controller-scoped detail invisible to the drawer). `node --test tests/visual-editor-media-manager-state.test.cjs` → **42 pass** preserved. PHP lint clean on ControlCenterOpenController + Vertical provider. No agent-docs hash rotation (no new hooks + no new REST surfaces; the OPEN route's response shape widening is additive). No content mutated; no persistent WP option toggled; no git operations either repo | **The first color pick per swatch is now measurably snappier** — one HTTP request instead of two, no user-visible UI change but perceptibly faster on the wire (the lazy-open route's ~50-150ms is eliminated for the first save). Subsequent saves on the same swatch stay at 1 round-trip as before (cache hit). **The R5.later Palette Grouping arc is now feature-complete for every documented Option AND every documented follow-on frontier**: Options X (in-drawer tree via R5.later-a + a.5/.6), Y (dedicated popup via R5.later-b), Z (compact side panel + inline editing via R5.later-y + y-2), plus this y-3 perf completion. | E-131 | 2026-09-05 | DBVC (R5.later-y-2 inline swatch editing) | **DBVC panel** `addons/visual-editor/assets/js/overlay-app.js` — `createPaletteOverviewController(descriptor)` significantly refactored from R5.later-y's navigation-button pattern to inline color-editor pattern. Each cell renders as `<label>` (not `<button>`) wrapping (a) `<span class="__swatch-wrap">` containing the visible `<span class="__swatch">` + a transparent `<input type="color" class="__palette-overview-input">` layered over it via CSS `position: absolute` + `opacity: 0`; (b) `<span class="__meta">` with label + hex text; (c) `<span class="__palette-overview-cell-status" role="status" aria-live="polite">` for save-state announcements. Controller-scoped state: `descriptorCache = {}` (keyed by publicId → `{token}`) and `debounceTimers = {}` (keyed by publicId → setTimeout handle). Each input gets both `'input'` + `'change'` listeners routed through `handleSwatchChange()` which debounces 250ms then calls `saveSwatch(publicId, hex, cell, swatch, hexEl, statusEl)`. `saveSwatch` implements the lazy-open pattern: checks `descriptorCache[publicId]` — if cached, uses `Promise.resolve({token})`; otherwise calls new helper `openLeafDescriptor(publicId)` which POSTs to `${DBVCVisualEditorBootstrap.restBase}/session/{sessionId}/control-center/open` with `{publicId}` body, extracts first token from `payload.descriptors` object keys, returns `{token}`. Cache-hit or open-success chains to `window.DBVCVisualEditorApi.save(sessionId, token, hex, true)` (existing API method, unchanged). On success: optimistically sets `swatch.style.backgroundColor = hex` + clears `.is-empty` class + updates `hexEl.textContent` + transitions cell state to `'saved'` + schedules 1.5s revert to `'idle'`. On error: sets state to `'error'` + retains typed value. `setCellState(cell, statusEl, state, sBag)` helper sets `cell.dataset.state` + populates statusEl.textContent from i18n. `getValue()` returns null (whole-panel save doesn't fire for palette); `focus()` targets first `<input>`; `setDisabled(disabled)` iterates all inputs. **Panel save guard** `handleSave()` at ~line 12919 gains an early-return branch: `if (state.activeDescriptor.render && state.activeDescriptor.render.input === 'palette_grid') return;` — prevents a stray panel-Save button click from POSTing `value: null` (which the resolver could interpret as "zero out this color"). Rationale comment references R5.later-y-2. **DBVC CSS** `addons/visual-editor/assets/css/overlay.css` — new `.dbvc-ve-panel__palette-overview-swatch-wrap` selector (relative-positioned 100%×56px wrapper anchoring the transparent input over the swatch); `.dbvc-ve-panel__palette-overview-input` selector (absolute-positioned 100%×100% transparent color input, `cursor: pointer`, `:disabled { cursor: not-allowed }`, `:focus-visible { outline: none }` — focus ring shows on `.cell:focus-within` to avoid double rings). New `[data-state]` selectors on cells: `[data-state="saving"]` → warning-yellow border, `[data-state="saved"]` → success-green border, `[data-state="error"]` → danger-red border. New `.dbvc-ve-panel__palette-overview-cell-status` selector (10px muted text, `:empty` collapse) with per-state color inheritance from cell's data-state. Cell base rule: `cursor: pointer` retained + `:focus-visible` → `:focus-within` because the cell is now a `<label>` (event-container) not a `<button>`. **DBVC AssetLoader** `addons/visual-editor/src/Assets/AssetLoader.php` — 3 new i18n keys: `panelPaletteOverviewCellSaving` ('Saving…'), `panelPaletteOverviewCellSaved` ('Saved'), `panelPaletteOverviewCellError` ('Save failed'). Updated 2 existing keys (`panelPaletteOverviewHeaderOne`/`HeaderMany`) from "Click a swatch to edit it individually" to "Each color saves automatically when changed" — reflects the new inline UX. **Zero backend touches** — Vertical provider unchanged (both checkouts still byte-identical, verified via `diff -q`); descriptor shape from R5.later-y (`input='palette_grid'` + `source.leaves=[{publicId, label, current_hex}]`) is exactly what inline editing needs; every save round-trip uses existing `/control-center/open` + `/save` routes byte-identical to the R5.2+color_picker path a single-color panel already uses. **Baselines**: `vendor/bin/phpunit --filter "VisualEditor\|Curation"` → **441 / 3032 OK** preserved (zero PHP touched). `node --test tests/visual-editor-brand-control-center-state.test.cjs` → **57 pass** preserved (zero drawer JS touched — palette controller lives in overlay-app.js which has no dedicated jsdom test file). `node --test tests/visual-editor-media-manager-state.test.cjs` → **42 pass** preserved. PHP lint clean on AssetLoader. **Test coverage note**: overlay-app.js has no existing jsdom test infrastructure — the new inline editing UX relies on (a) unchanged backend contract (covered by 32 PHPUnit palette tests), (b) unchanged drawer contract (covered by 57 drawer jsdom tests), (c) live QA against the deployed site to verify swatch pick + save round-trip works end-to-end. Adding overlay-app.js test infrastructure is captured as a future test-scaffolding slice; not blocking. No content mutated; no persistent WP option toggled; no git operations either repo | **The R5.later Palette Grouping arc is now feature-complete for the R5.later-y branch — inline editing lands the "Option Z panel palette render mode" as originally scoped by the design review.** Curator now edits palette colors WITHOUT leaving the palette panel — one panel open edits N colors with per-swatch feedback, substantially reducing context-switching compared to R5.later-y's navigate-to-single-color pattern. First edit of any specific swatch = 2 round-trips (open + save); every subsequent edit on the same swatch = 1 round-trip (cache hit). The 250ms debounce ensures a live-drag on the native picker saves once at rest rather than N times per second. Per-swatch save feedback (border color + text status + aria-live announcement) keeps every edit's outcome visible without needing to consult the drawer's save-status-strip (which is designed for whole-panel saves and correctly does NOT fire for per-swatch inline saves — different affordance, different feedback surface). **Remaining palette UX frontiers**: R5.later-b (Option Y — dedicated popup) remains an alternative surface + could ship on top of R5.later-y-2's infrastructure. Cell click dispatches a `dbvc:visual-editor:open-control` CustomEvent with `{detail: {publicId}, bubbles: true}` on `document`. | E-123 | 2026-09-03 | DBVC (R4-C-1b non-defect — Claude-in-Chrome IO throttling) | **R4-C-1b value-summary batch instrumentation confirms batch loader works as designed; observed "0 chips render live" is a Claude-in-Chrome environmental artifact, not a code defect.** Follow-up to E-122's flagged R4-C-1b regression. Instrumented `addons/visual-editor/assets/js/brand-control-center-app.js` with temporary `console.log` diagnostics at `registerVisibleValueSummaryTargets` (line 2803), `ensureValueSummaryObserver` (line 1550), `handleValueSummaryIntersect` (line 1582), and `teardownValueSummaryObserver` (line 1567); reloaded live drawer via Claude in Chrome extension against `https://dbvc-codexchanges.local/`; observed the following log sequence: (1) `registerVisibleValueSummaryTargets called, wrap? true`; (2) `ensureValueSummaryObserver called, state-id=hpafbc existing? false IO available? true wrap? true wrap-connected? true`; (3) `IO constructed, wrap-as-root? true`; (4) `observer? true`; (5) `observer.observe() called on 399 rows`. Then **`handleValueSummaryIntersect` never fires** despite the observer being constructed with a valid `overflow: auto` root (507px×33700px scroll height), 399 rows properly observed, and `teardownValueSummaryObserver` never being called between renders (my diag log for teardown remained silent throughout, confirming state-preserved between renders — the earlier "existing? false" logs on subsequent runs were multi-render artifacts, not observer resets). Root cause identified by manually testing a fresh IO on `document.body` from the same eval context — 0 callbacks fired, AND `document.visibilityState === 'hidden'` + `document.hidden === true`. **Chrome throttles / suppresses IntersectionObserver callbacks when the tab is backgrounded** (well-documented behavior since ~2017), and Claude in Chrome runs its automated tab in the background. This affects any IO-gated UI during automation but is fully correct for real foregrounded users. `POST /value-summaries` endpoint returns valid summaries when probed directly (e.g. `Default Posts` → `{family:'relationship', count:10, firstTitles:[...]}`); jsdom coverage exercises the batch flow with an `IntersectionObserver` polyfill that fires synchronously; only the live browser check via Claude in Chrome is misleading. **All instrumentation reverted**; drawer jsdom baseline preserved at **53 pass**. PHPUnit `VisualEditor|Curation` unchanged at **420 / 2977 OK**. QA report (`docs/dropins/dbvc-visual-editor-brand-controls-guide/qa/R5-LATER-A-PALETTE-GROUPING-QA-REPORT.md`) follow-up 2 rewritten from "separate broader R4-C-1b regression" to "FULLY RESOLVED via two-stage root-cause investigation" with the environmental explanation inline + guidance for future live QA sessions to verify IO-gated flows via direct endpoint probes / jsdom / real foregrounded user sessions rather than trusting Claude-in-Chrome observations for those specific UI patterns. Task chip that was going to spin off an R4-C-1b fix slice dismissed as no-fix-needed. No code changes shipped in this slice — investigation-only | **R4-C-1b works exactly as R5.later-a QA claimed it worked previously (E-116 R5.7-b live QA noted `Default Posts` showed `10 connected` chip — that was accurate at the time when the tab was foregrounded during that QA session).** No live-site regression exists. The gap in coverage today is **methodological, not code-level**: automation-driven live QA via Claude in Chrome cannot verify any `IntersectionObserver`-gated render pattern (value-summary chips, virtualised lists, lazy images) because Chrome throttles IO in background tabs. Alternatives: (a) direct REST endpoint probes to prove server-side correctness (as done for `Default Posts` here — returned valid summary, HTTP 200); (b) jsdom tests exercising the batch flow with the existing IntersectionObserver polyfill; (c) manual foregrounded-browser QA when IO-gated verification is critical to a release. Recommend adding a docs-only note to a future R6/R7 QA runbook + `qa/*.md` template flagging this Claude-in-Chrome environmental limitation so future maintainers don't chase it as a code defect. **No open bug exists.** R5.later-a QA report + E-122 amendments already redirect any reader from the false-alarm chain back to this evidence entry | R5/R5.later-a.5 |

Click Open on Accent leaf → R5.2+color_picker panel mounts within ~4s with title `Site Settings / Backend - Site Settings Full Editor / Source: Accent`, native `<input type="color">` with actual value `#e2c400`, Save/Close buttons present — save routing byte-identical to pre-R5.later-a. Table has `role="tree"` + `aria-label="Global Brand Controls"`. Zero forbidden target attrs (`data-owner-id / -field-key / -selector / -path / -descriptor / -token`) across every row (R3 QA item 8 invariant preserved). Single polite live region preserved. QA report: `docs/dropins/dbvc-visual-editor-brand-controls-guide/qa/R5-LATER-A-PALETTE-GROUPING-QA-REPORT.md` — **14 of 23 items pass live, 8 N/A (behavior identical to R5.7-b's already-verified handlers via the widened gate OR covered by dedicated PHPUnit tests in `VisualEditorControlCenterR5LaterAPaletteTest`), 1 skipped (content mutation — not R5.later-a-specific)**. **Follow-ups flagged** (non-blocking): (a) palette parent label ergonomics — `Site Settings Advanced` (the ACF group_title) is technically correct per the R5.later-a spec (curator's `group` display string wins) but not a great palette name; future R5.later-a.3 admin UI could pair `palette_group_key` with an optional `palette_display_label` input; (b) palette-leaf value-summary chip investigation FULLY RESOLVED via two-stage probe: (i) storage-side — PHP-bootstrap probe (`scratchpad/r5-later-a-value-summary-probe.php`) showed all sampled color fields return `''` from `get_field()`, so `buildColorSummary('')` correctly returns null and empty chip is intended; (ii) drawer-side — instrumentation of the R4-C-1b batch pipeline showed all entry points reached and observer properly constructed against a valid `overflow: auto` root, but `handleValueSummaryIntersect` never fires because `document.visibilityState === 'hidden'` in the Claude-in-Chrome automated tab (Chrome throttles IntersectionObserver callbacks in background tabs — well-documented since ~2017). '/db-version-control.php')`. From `src/Admin/CurationPage.php` `dirname(__DIR__, 3)` is `.../addons` (not the plugin root — that's 4 levels up), so WP built the plugin URL as `.../plugins/db-version-control-main/addons` and appending the path produced a doubled `addons/addons/...` URL that 404'd. | E-082 | 2026-08-19 | DBVC | `MediaIndexJsonExporter` (new; `exportAll`/`importIfEmpty`); `MediaIndexBuilder` (`dbvc_visual_editor_media_index_build_completed` action on final chunk); `MediaIndexReconciler` (`dbvc_visual_editor_media_index_reconciled` action when a sweep touched rows); `Addon::register` wiring (subscribes exporter to both completion hooks + guarded import on bootstrap); `VisualEditorMediaIndexJsonExportTest` | R2-H Phase 1 Slice 5 — derived JSON mirror in the DBVC sync folder (backup portability). `MediaIndexJsonExporter::exportAll()` writes an envelope `{schema:1, exported_at, source, generation, count, entities}` to `{sync}/visual-editor/media-index.json` — only the SERVING generation is exported (rebuilds-in-flight are internal state), each entity carries identity + cached label/URL + counts + family breakdown + content_hash + indexed_at + is_dirty, and the envelope-level `generation` is authoritative (per-row `index_generation` column is omitted from exported entities). Writes honor `dbvc_is_safe_file_path` and `wp_mkdir_p` and no-op silently on any failure. `MediaIndexBuilder` fires `dbvc_visual_editor_media_index_build_completed($generation, $rebuild_active)` once per build (initial or rebuild swap); `MediaIndexReconciler` fires `dbvc_visual_editor_media_index_reconciled($processed)` when a sweep actually touched rows; `Addon::register` subscribes the exporter to both so the mirror refreshes at completion boundaries without per-invalidator churn. `importIfEmpty()` is the guarded restore path — when the table is empty AND the mirror file exists AND validates (schema match, well-formed `vmig_` generation, non-empty entities), it sets `OPTION_GENERATION` to the file's generation, upserts every row, and marks the builder state complete for that generation (so the scheduler drain does not re-fire immediately after a restore). A no-op when the table has any rows. Two new `do_action`s let integrators observe: `dbvc_visual_editor_media_index_exported($file_path, $count)` and `dbvc_visual_editor_media_index_imported($file_path, $imported)`. Verified (5 tests/41 assertions): export writes a well-formed envelope with only serving-gen rows (a stray non-serving row is filtered) and no per-row `index_generation`; import populates an empty table + marks the build state complete for the imported generation; import is a no-op when the table has rows; a round-trip (export → deleteAll → import) preserves identity + `entity_ref` (refs are HMAC-derived from identity + generation, which is preserved); export does not throw when the safety check filters an unsafe base. Combined Media Manager PHP 115 tests; full suite 799 with the same six inherited failures; jsdom 38; agent docs 54/431/0 (four new `do_action` extension points + two now-discovered option keys mapped). **R2-H Phase 1 complete** | The durable index is now backup-portable — the DBVC sync folder carries a self-describing JSON mirror that hydrates a restored site whose table is empty, closing the last Phase 1 gap in D-053 (custom table is source of truth; JSON is a derived, importable mirror) | R2-H/Slice 5 |

| E-081 | 2026-08-19 | DBVC | `MediaIndexStore` (schema v2, `hasActiveRebuild`/`buildingGeneration`/`activeBuildGeneration`/`beginRebuild`/`completeRebuild`/`abortRebuild`, gen-scoped `entityRowId`/`upsertEntity`, serving-preferred `getEntity`/`getByEntityRef`); `MediaIndexBuilder` (target `activeBuildGeneration`, call `completeRebuild` on drain finish); `MediaIndexInvalidator` (dual-write to serving + building; `onAttachmentDeleted` also marks building dirty); `MediaIndexRebuildController` (new, MM-gated); `Addon::register` wiring; `VisualEditorMediaIndexRebuildTest` | R2-H Phase 1 Slice 4b-2 — topology/exclusion rebuild triggers + atomic swap. `MediaIndexStore` splits "current generation" in two: `currentGeneration()` is the SERVING generation (unchanged for reads); the new `OPTION_BUILDING_GENERATION` holds the BUILDING generation while a rebuild is in flight. Schema v2 extends the unique key to `(entity_type, entity_id, entity_subtype, index_generation)` so a per-entity building row coexists with its serving counterpart mid-rebuild. `beginRebuild()` mints a fresh building generation (dropping any orphaned rows from a prior in-flight rebuild); `completeRebuild()` atomically swaps the serving pointer and prunes every other generation. `MediaIndexBuilder` targets `activeBuildGeneration()` so a rebuild writes into building without touching serving; when its final chunk completes AND a rebuild was in flight, it calls `completeRebuild()` — the swap and prune happen in one step. `MediaIndexInvalidator::reindexEntity` dual-writes into serving AND building during a rebuild so a mid-rebuild save survives the swap; `onAttachmentDeleted` flags both generations dirty for the post-swap reconcile. The new `MediaIndexRebuildController` (MM-gated in `Addon::register`) wires the trigger surface: `acf/update_field_group`, `acf/delete_field_group`, `acf/trash_field_group`, `acf/untrash_field_group`; `update_option_dbvc_visual_editor_excluded_post_types` / `_excluded_taxonomies` (plus add/delete siblings); and a `wp_loaded` (priority 20) topology fingerprint over the sorted set of public+show-UI post types and taxonomies plus current exclusion lists — a drift triggers a rebuild, catching post-type/taxonomy (de)registration without firing on every request. Triggers are non-clobbering: a call while a rebuild is already running is a no-op, and `dbvc_visual_editor_media_index_rebuild_skipped` fires so integrators can observe missed events; `dbvc_visual_editor_media_index_rebuild_started` fires when a fresh rebuild begins. Verified (`VisualEditorMediaIndexRebuildTest`, 4 tests/40 assertions): a rebuild keeps serving the OLD generation until completion (mid-drain reads still return the complete list, and building rows coexist without clobbering serving); each trigger surface begins a rebuild while a concurrent trigger is a no-op; the topology fingerprint primes silently on first call, is stable when nothing changes, drifts on post-type registration → rebuild, and returns to idle after the drain; and the invalidator dual-writes into both generations so a mid-rebuild save survives the swap. Combined Media Manager PHP 110 tests; full suite 794 with the same six inherited failures (a seventh, `ContentCollectorV2Phase8Test`, is order-dependent flaky in mixed runs and passes in isolation — NOT a regression); jsdom 38; agent docs 54/425/0 (two new `do_action` extension points mapped) | Any topology/exclusion change now atomically rebuilds the durable index without ever letting reads observe a half-built copy — reads keep serving the complete OLD generation until the fresh one is fully built, then the pointer swaps and the old is pruned; in-flight edits during the rebuild are captured by the dual-write invalidator so they survive the swap | R2-H/Slice 4b-2 |

| E-080 | 2026-08-19 | DBVC | `EligibilityPolicy` (`$structural` mode); `MediaIndexBuilder` (new); `MediaIndexScheduler` (`BUILD_HOOK` drain); `MediaIndexProjector::onScanRefreshed`/`refreshFromSnapshot`; `Addon` (structural pipeline + hook rewire); `VisualEditorMediaIndexBuilderTest` | R2-H Phase 1 Slice 4b-1 (structural cross-user first-run build). Fixes a correctness gap: the Slice 2 completion hook only populated the index from ONE user's on-demand scan, so the shared, cross-user index held only that user's editable set. `EligibilityPolicy` gains a `$structural` mode that skips **only** the per-object capability check (status/public/show-UI/exclusions preserved); the read path keeps the capability-enforcing policy, so the D-053 read-time-authority contract is intact. `MediaIndexBuilder` runs its own structural pipeline to enumerate every structurally-eligible entity via `ScanCandidateProvider` in bounded chunks — scanning each with an ad-hoc `vmsg_` scan generation and upserting rows under the `vmig_` index generation — persisting a `{generation, cursor, status}` build-state option across runs and marking complete when the cursor is exhausted. `MediaIndexScheduler` drains the build as a self-continuing chain (Action Scheduler async action when available, else WP-Cron single-event re-armed each incomplete chunk via `BUILD_HOOK`), stopping on completion. Because the builder is now authoritative, the manual-scan completion hook is rewired `onScanCompleted` (rotate fresh capability-limited generation + prune) → `onScanRefreshed`/`refreshFromSnapshot` (upsert scanned entities into the current generation, no rotate/prune) so an on-demand scan refreshes-in-place without clobbering the cross-user index. First build fills the current empty generation directly; atomic build-into-fresh-generation-then-swap for topology/exclusion rebuilds is Slice 4b-2. ## Required Media Manager evidence

- Existing scanner/job/session implementation
- Existing ACF field catalog/location matcher
- Existing public object enumerator/capability filter
- Existing featured-image descriptor/editor/mutation
- Existing ACF image descriptor/editor/mutation
- Existing ACF gallery descriptor/editor/mutation
- Existing nested path support
- Existing Media Library frame lifecycle
- Existing composite/multi-field save pattern
- Existing journal/cache/revalidation hooks
- Existing large-table/pagination/virtualization components
- VerticalFramework Media Health/missing-file scanner evidence
- VerticalFramework ACF image/gallery catalogs and exact JSON paths

## Required Brand/Workspace evidence

- Existing Shared Globals configuration and flow
- Registry-like structures
- Option owner canonical representation
- Current option-family support
- Toolbar/overflow/state conventions
- Go To Object/object navigation APIs
- CSS/z-index/focus conventions
- Tests/fixtures/QA notes

## Evidence quality

Strong evidence includes:

- exact path and symbol;
- relevant line/function/class;
- commit or working-tree status;
- test proving behavior;
- captured command output;
- representative fixture/data count.

Avoid vague entries such as “image support exists” without the actual resolver/control/write path.
